Clause / section / page: 10.3.1.3 / Part I-A Chapter 10 - Ship communication / 28

Original source text:
Two-way on-scene and SAR coordination communication capability in ships shall include: voice and/or data communications with relevant rescue coordination centres; and equipment for voice communications with aircraft on 121.5 and 123.1 MHz.
Normalized requirement:
SAR_communication_capability shall include rescue_coordination_centre_voice_or_data = true AND aircraft_voice_frequency_support_MHz containing both 121.5 and 123.1.
Required inputs: Communication capability declaration and supported frequency list

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/imo-batch-j/IMO-095-J-P02/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:rescueCoordinationCentreVoiceOrData true ;
    nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ex:freq1,
        ex:freq2,
        ex:freq3 .

ex:freq1 a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 123.1 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MegaHZ .

ex:freq2 a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 121.5 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MegaHZ .

ex:freq3 a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 130.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MegaHZ .


```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix g: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

g:IMO_095_Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:searchAndRescueCommunicationCapability ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall declare search and rescue communication capability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:rescueCoordinationCentreVoiceOrData ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide voice or data communications with relevant rescue coordination centres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ;
        sh:minCount 2 ;
        sh:node [
            a sh:NodeShape ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaHZ>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship shall support aircraft voice communications on both 121.5 MHz and 123.1 MHz."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The supported aircraft voice frequencies shall include both 121.5 MHz and 123.1 MHz." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ?q121 .
                        ?q121 qudt:numericValue ?v121 .
                        ?q121 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaHZ> .
                        FILTER (?v121 >= 121.5 && ?v121 <= 121.5)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ?q123 .
                        ?q123 qudt:numericValue ?v123 .
                        ?q123 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaHZ> .
                        FILTER (?v123 >= 123.1 && ?v123 <= 123.1)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
gen:IMO_095_Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:searchAndRescueCommunicationCapability ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall declare search and rescue communication capability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:rescueCoordinationCentreVoiceOrData ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide voice or data communications with relevant rescue coordination centres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ;
        sh:minCount 2 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO_095_FrequencyQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "The ship shall support aircraft voice communications on both 121.5 MHz and 123.1 MHz."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The supported aircraft voice frequencies shall include 121.5 MHz." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ?quantity .
                    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                              qudt:unit unit:MegaHZ .
                    FILTER(?value = 121.5)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The supported aircraft voice frequencies shall include 123.1 MHz." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:supportedAircraftVoiceFrequency ?quantity .
                    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                              qudt:unit unit:MegaHZ .
                    FILTER(?value = 123.1)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO_095_FrequencyQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
```

Clause / section / page: 4.4.3 / Longitudinal frames / 20
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-048-20260915T130712002188Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The section modulus of a longitudinal frame shall be calculated using Z = f_4*p*h*l^2/(4*m*sigma_y)*10^6 [cm^3] (4.9). The effective shear area shall be A = sqrt(3)*f_4*f_5*p*h*l/(2*sigma_y)*10^4 [cm^2] (4.10). The bracket shear area shall not be included in the actual frame shear area. Use f_4 = 1 - 0.2*h/s, f_5 = 2.16, p from 4.2.2, h from 4.2.1, s as frame spacing, l as total frame span, m = 13.3 for a continuous beam with brackets unless a smaller factor is required by significantly different boundary conditions, and sigma_y from 4.3.2.
Normalized requirement:
Compute f_4 = 1 - 0.2*h/s, Z_cm3 = f_4*p_MPa*h_m*l_m^2/(4*m*sigma_y_N_mm2)*10^6, and A_cm2 = sqrt(3)*f_4*2.16*p_MPa*h_m*l_m/(2*sigma_y_N_mm2)*10^4. Use m = 13.3 for a continuous beam with brackets unless the actual end field requires a smaller factor. Exclude bracket area from actual_frame_shear_area.
Required inputs: yieldStrength_MPa, sectionModulusZ, shearAreaA

```turtle
            ] ;

                sh:hasValue false

                sh:path nltl:frameBoundaryConditionType ;

    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The frame distribution factor, section modulus, and required shear area must match the declared direct calculations within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, with an absolute tolerance of 1e-9 near zero." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                      nltl:spacing ?spacingNode ;
                      nltl:span ?spanNode ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?momentNode ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalFrameLoadDistributionFactorF4 ?f4Node ;
                      nltl:sectionModulus ?zNode ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?aNode .

                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?spacingNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?l .
                ?momentNode qudt:numericValue ?m .
                ?yieldNode qudt:numericValue ?sigma .
                ?f4Node qudt:numericValue ?f4 .
                ?zNode qudt:numericValue ?z .
                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .

                BIND ((1 - (0.2 * ?h / ?s)) AS ?expectedF4)
                BIND ((?expectedF4 * ?p * ?h * ?l * ?l / (4 * ?m * ?sigma) * 1000000) AS ?expectedZ)
                BIND ((1.7320508075688772 * ?expectedF4 * 2.16 * ?p * ?h * ?l / (2 * ?sigma) * 10000) AS ?expectedA)

                FILTER (
                    (ABS(?f4 - ?expectedF4) > (1e-6 * ABS(?expectedF4)) &&
                     ABS(?f4 - ?expectedF4) > 1e-9)
                    ||
                    (ABS(?z - ?expectedZ) > (1e-6 * ABS(?expectedZ)) &&
                     ABS(?z - ?expectedZ) > 1e-9)
                    ||
                    (ABS(?a - ?expectedA) > (1e-6 * ABS(?expectedA)) &&
                     ABS(?a - ?expectedA) > 1e-9)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/CentiM3> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-LongitudinalFrameShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:longitudinalFrame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameBoundaryConditionType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:continuousBeamWithBracketsFrameCondition
            nltl:bulkCarrierTopWingTankFrameCondition
            nltl:multiDeckOrStringerContinuousFrameCondition
            nltl:singleDeckTankTopToMainDeckFrameCondition
            nltl:twoDeckFrameCondition
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:significantlyDifferentBoundaryConditions ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-MegaPAQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceLoadHeight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-MeterQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:spacing ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-MeterQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:span ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-MeterQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameMomentFactorM ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-UnitlessQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-MegaPAQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalFrameLoadDistributionFactorF4 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-UnitlessQuantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sectionModulus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-CentiM3Quantity>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-048-CentiM2Quantity>
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a continuous beam with brackets without significantly different boundary conditions, frameMomentFactorM must equal 13.3." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:frameBoundaryConditionType nltl:continuousBeamWithBracketsFrameCondition ;
                      nltl:significantlyDifferentBoundaryConditions false ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?mNode .
                ?mNode qudt:numericValue ?m .
                FILTER (?m < 13.3 || ?m > 13.3)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The longitudinal frame calculation results do not satisfy the declared f4, section modulus, and required shear area formulas." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pNode ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?hNode ;
                      nltl:spacing ?sNode ;
                      nltl:span ?lNode ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?mNode ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?sigmaNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalFrameLoadDistributionFactorF4 ?f4Node ;
                      nltl:sectionModulus ?zNode ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?aNode .
                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?hNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?sNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?lNode qudt:numericValue ?l .
                ?mNode qudt:numericValue ?m .
                ?sigmaNode qudt:numericValue ?sigma .
                ?f4Node qudt:numericValue ?f4 .
                ?zNode qudt:numericValue ?z .
                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .
                BIND ((1 - (0.2 * ?h / ?s)) AS ?expectedF4)
                BIND (((1 - (0.2 * ?h / ?s)) * ?p * ?h * ?l * ?l / (4 * ?m * ?sigma) * 1000000) AS ?expectedZ)
                BIND ((1.7320508075688772 * (1 - (0.2 * ?h / ?s)) * 2.16 * ?p * ?h * ?l / (2 * ?sigma) * 10000) AS ?expectedA)
                FILTER (
                    ABS(?f4 - ?expectedF4) > (ABS(?expectedF4) * 0.000001 + 0.000000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?z - ?expectedZ) > (ABS(?expectedZ) * 0.000001 + 0.000000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?a - ?expectedA) > (ABS(?expectedA) * 0.000001 + 0.000000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
The design vertical ice bending moment along the hull girder is to be taken as M_I = 0.1*C_m*L_UI*sin(gamma_stem)^(-0.2)*F_IB [MNm]. Use C_m = 0.0 at the aft end, 1.0 between 0.5*L_UI and 0.7*L_UI from aft, 0.3 at 0.95*L_UI from aft, and 0.0 at the forward end; intermediate values are to be determined by linear interpolation.
Normalized requirement:

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF045_FrameOrdinaryScantlingsShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:ordinaryFrameScantlingsUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "ordinaryFrameScantlingsUsed must be a boolean when supplied."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameSpanWithinIceStrengtheningZone ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:span ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Ordinary frame scantlings may be used only when the frame span within the ice-strengthening zone is less than 15% of the frame span, with both lengths supplied in metres." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:ordinaryFrameScantlingsUsed true .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:frameSpanWithinIceStrengtheningZone ?iceQuantity .
                    ?iceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?iceValue ;
                                  qudt:unit unit:M .
                    $this nltl:span ?spanQuantity .
                    ?spanQuantity qudt:numericValue ?spanValue ;
                                  qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (?iceValue < (0.15 * ?spanValue))
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:minCount 1

                $this nltl:ordinaryFrameScantlingsUsed true .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-045_FrameSpanIceZone> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameSpanWithinIceStrengtheningZone ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:span ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:ordinaryFrameScantlingsUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Ordinary frame scantlings may be used only when less than 15% of the frame span is situated within the ice-strengthening zone." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:ordinaryFrameScantlingsUsed ?used .
                FILTER (?used = true)

                $this nltl:frameSpanWithinIceStrengtheningZone ?withinQuantity .
                $this nltl:span ?spanQuantity .
                ?withinQuantity qudt:numericValue ?withinValue .
                ?spanQuantity qudt:numericValue ?spanValue .

                FILTER (?withinValue >= 0.15 * ?spanValue)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this

                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK2/qudt:numericValue ?k2 ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter/qudt:numericValue ?d ;
                      nltl:propellerImmersionFunction/qudt:numericValue ?f ;
                      nltl:propellerShaftCentrelineDepth/qudt:numericValue ?h0 .

                BIND (((?h0 - ?h) / (?d / 2)) - 1 AS ?expectedF)
                BIND (
                    IF(?expectedF < 0,
                       0.8 - ?expectedF,
                       IF(?expectedF <= 1,
                          0.8 - (0.4 * ?expectedF),

                )

                FILTER (
                    ?f != ?expectedF
                    ||
                    ?k2 != ?expectedK2
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerShaftCentrelineDepth ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The immersion function and ice load cycle coefficient do not satisfy the declared formulas; calculated decimal results use a relative tolerance of 1e-6 plus an absolute tolerance of 1e-9." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceThickness ?hIce ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK2 ?k2 ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter ?diameter ;
                      nltl:propellerImmersionFunction ?fReported ;
                      nltl:propellerShaftCentrelineDepth ?h0 .

                ?hIce qudt:numericValue ?hIceValue .
                ?k2 qudt:numericValue ?k2Value .
                ?diameter qudt:numericValue ?diameterValue .
                ?fReported qudt:numericValue ?fReportedValue .
                ?h0 qudt:numericValue ?h0Value .

                BIND ((2 * (?h0Value - ?hIceValue) / ?diameterValue) - 1 AS ?fExpected)
                BIND (
                    IF(
                        ?fExpected < 0,
                        0.8 - ?fExpected,
                        IF(
                            ?fExpected <= 1,
                            0.8 - (0.4 * ?fExpected),
                            IF(
                                ?fExpected <= 2.5,
                                0.6 - (0.2 * ?fExpected),
                                0.1
                            )
                        )
                    ) AS ?k2Expected
                )

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?fReportedValue - ?fExpected) >
                    (ABS(?fExpected) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?k2Value - ?k2Expected) >
                    (ABS(?k2Expected) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                        ABS(?fExpected) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                        ABS(?fExpected) * 0.000001,
                        0.000000001
                    ) AS ?fTolerance
                )
                BIND(
                    IF(
                        ABS(?kExpected) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                        ABS(?kExpected) * 0.000001,
                        0.000000001
                    ) AS ?kTolerance

                    ?f > (?fExpected + ?fTolerance) ||
                    ?k < (?kExpected - ?kTolerance) ||
                    ?k > (?kExpected + ?kTolerance)
                )
```

Category B ship means a ship not included in category A, designed for operation in polar waters in at least thin first-year ice, which may include old ice inclusions.

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO002ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must declare its design ice condition." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCategoryValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must declare its Polar Code category." ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A ship designed to operate in at least thin first-year ice with possible old-ice inclusions, and not declared as category A, must be category B." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceCondition ?designIceCondition .
                FILTER (
                    ?designIceCondition = nltl:thinFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions
                    || ?designIceCondition = nltl:mediumFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:shipCategory nltl:polarShipCategoryA .
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:shipCategory nltl:polarShipCategoryB .
                }
            }
        """ ;
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-002-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A single design ice condition is required to determine applicability of the category rule."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCategoryValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A single declared Polar Code ship category is required."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:thinFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB
                    nltl:iceFreeIceCondition
                    nltl:mediumFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions
                    nltl:openWaterIceCondition
                    nltl:otherWatersIceCondition
                )
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "A ship not in category A and designed for at least thin first-year ice with possible old-ice inclusions shall be category B." .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gen:IMO002ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must declare its design ice condition."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCategoryValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must declare its Polar Code category."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A ship designed to operate in at least thin first-year ice with possible old-ice inclusions, and not in category A, must be category B." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceCondition
                    nltl:thinFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions .
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                FILTER (
                    ?category != nltl:polarShipCategoryA &&
                    ?category != nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
The upper end of the strengthened part of a main frame and of an intermediate ice frame shall be attached to a deck, top or bottom plating of a tank or an ice stringer (section 4.5). Where a frame terminates above a deck or a stringer which is situated at or above the upper limit of the ice belt (section 4.3.1), the part above the deck or stringer may have the scantlings required by the classification society for an non ice-strengthened ship and the upper end of an intermediate frame may be connected to the adjacent frames by a horizontal member with the same scantlings as the main frame.
Normalized requirement:
The upper end of the strengthened part of a main frame and of an intermediate ice frame shall be attached to a deck, top or bottom plating of a tank or an ice stringer (section 4.5). Where a frame terminates above a deck or a stringer which is situated at or above the upper limit of the ice belt (section 4.3.1), the part above the deck or stringer may have the scantlings required by the classification society for an non ice-strengthened ship and the upper end of an intermediate frame may be connected to the adjacent frames by a horizontal member with the same scantlings as the main frame.

}
Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-046-UpperEndAttachmentShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A main frame or intermediate ice frame must have a strengthened part whose upper end is attached to a deck structure, tank boundary plating, or ice stringer." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this rdf:type ?frameType .
                FILTER (
                    ?frameType IN (
                        nltl:mainFrame,
                        nltl:intermediateIceFrame
                    )
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasStrengthenedPart ?strengthenedPart .
                    ?strengthenedPart rdf:type nltl:frameStrengthenedPart .

                    $this nltl:hasUpperEnd ?upperEnd .
                    ?upperEnd rdf:type nltl:frameEnd .
                    ?upperEnd nltl:hasAttachedSupportingStructure ?supportingStructure .

                    ?supportingStructure rdf:type ?supportingStructureType .
                    FILTER (
                        ?supportingStructureType IN (
                            nltl:deckStructure,
                            nltl:tankBoundaryPlating,
                            nltl:iceStringer
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
(ix) Ship structures that are not directly subjected to ice loads may still experience inertial loads of stowed cargo and equipment resulting from ship/ice interaction. These inertial loads, based on accelerations determined by the Classification Society, are to be considered in the design of these structures. The parameters defining the glancing impact load characteristics are reflected in the Class Factors listed in Table 1 and Table 2.

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?

Csp is a non-dimensional parameter taking account of the spindle arm Cfex is a non-dimensional parameter taking account of the reduction of the blade failure force at the location of the maximum spindle torque. Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
